6041 — bottle weighted romanian deadlift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6041 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A hip hinge holding a weighted bottle in each hand. It trains the hamstrings and glutes with light load.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with feet hip-width apart, holding a bottle in each hand in front of your thighs.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 6041.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Legs Feet

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 6041.json#/instructions/0

Equipment Contacts

Retain every explicit contact in equipment_scene.contacts. Use only the documented prop choices: Weighted bottle pair. For this standing proposal, the support foot or feet contact a level ground plane; that ground plane is an authored scene choice when the source does not name it. Do not assign unknown apparatus dimensions, pad settings, attachment heights or load values.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 6041.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with feet hip-width apart, holding a bottle in each hand in front of your thighs. 6041.json#/instructions/0
  2. Soften your knees and push your hips back. 6041.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower the bottles along your legs with a flat back until you feel your hamstrings stretch. 6041.json#/instructions/2
  4. Drive your hips forward to stand tall. 6041.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your shoulders back and your core braced. 6041.json#/instructions/4

6042 — bottle weighted single leg romanian deadlift

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6042 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A one-legged hip hinge holding weighted bottles. It works the hamstrings and glutes and challenges balance.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand on one leg holding a bottle in each hand, with a slight bend in the knee.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 6042.json#/instructions/0

Arms Hands

Stand on one leg holding a bottle in each hand, with a slight bend in the knee.

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 6042.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand on one leg holding a bottle in each hand, with a slight bend in the knee. 6042.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hinge at the hips and extend the free leg straight behind you. 6042.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower the bottles toward the floor with a flat back. 6042.json#/instructions/2
  4. Squeeze your glute to return to standing. 6042.json#/instructions/3
  5. Finish your reps, then switch legs. 6042.json#/instructions/4

6043 — bottle weighted swing

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6043 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A hip-driven swing with a weighted bottle held in both hands. It trains the glutes and hamstrings explosively.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand with feet wider than your hips, holding a bottle with both hands.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 6043.json#/instructions/0

authored_scene_blocking; source verified: false. Scene support follows the source posture; unsupported inventory candidates are excluded from this proposal, not silently asserted. 6043.json#/instructions/0

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand with feet wider than your hips, holding a bottle with both hands. 6043.json#/instructions/0
  2. Hinge back and let the bottle swing between your legs. 6043.json#/instructions/1
  3. Snap your hips forward and let the bottle swing to chest height. 6043.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your arms relaxed, the hips do the work. 6043.json#/instructions/3
  5. Let it fall back and hinge into the next rep. 6043.json#/instructions/4

6044 — bottle weighted front squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6044 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A squat holding weighted bottles at shoulder height. It works the quads and glutes and keeps your torso upright.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Hold a bottle in each hand at your shoulders, elbows pointing forward. Stand with feet shoulder-width apart. Sit your hips down and back until your thighs are about parallel.

source_cue_reuse; source verified: true. 6044.json#/instructions/0, 6044.json#/instructions/1, 6044.json#/instructions/2

  1. Hold a bottle in each hand at your shoulders, elbows pointing forward. 6044.json#/instructions/0
  2. Stand with feet shoulder-width apart. 6044.json#/instructions/1
  3. Sit your hips down and back until your thighs are about parallel. 6044.json#/instructions/2
  4. Keep your chest up and knees tracking over your toes. 6044.json#/instructions/3
  5. Press through your heels to stand. 6044.json#/instructions/4

6045 — bottle weighted sumo squat

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6045 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A wide-stance squat holding a weighted bottle between your legs. It hits the glutes and inner thighs.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

6048 — bottle weighted rear lunge

Index · Full JSON chunk download (.gz; find ID 6048 after decompression) · Source record

Source completeness: needs_review. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A lunge stepping backward while holding bottles. It is easy on the knees and trains the glutes and quads.

Source completeness: source_complete_for_storyboard. Storyboard proposal: proposal_ready_for_review. Pose class: standing.

A single-arm snatch that takes a dumbbell from the floor to overhead in one motion. This version uses the left arm.

Proposed staging

Starting Position

Stand over a dumbbell with your feet shoulder-width apart. Grip it with your left hand and hinge at the hips.

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Stand over a dumbbell with your feet shoulder-width apart. 6152.json#/instructions/0
  2. Grip it with your left hand and hinge at the hips. 6152.json#/instructions/1
  3. Drive up with your legs and hips. 6152.json#/instructions/2
  4. Pull the dumbbell up close to your body and punch it overhead. 6152.json#/instructions/3
  5. Lock out the arm. 6152.json#/instructions/4
  6. Lower the weight back to the floor with control. 6152.json#/instructions/5

Complete ordered source instructions

  1. Lie on a bench and hold the bar with a close, shoulder-width grip. 6271.json#/instructions/0
  2. Lift your feet off the floor and stretch your legs out in front of you. 6271.json#/instructions/1
  3. Lower the bar to your lower chest under control. 6271.json#/instructions/2
  4. Press the bar back up until your arms are straight. 6271.json#/instructions/3
  5. Keep your back flat on the bench and your core tight throughout. 6271.json#/instructions/4
